    b = cities[ci];
    if (baseOfficers(b).length > 0 || F.ap < AP.move) continue;
    var coming = G.officers.some(function (o) { return o.st === 'travel' && o.f === f && o.dest === b.id; });
    if (coming) continue;
    var src = null, sd = 1e9;
    cities.forEach(function (c2) { if (c2 === b) return; var n = idleOfficers(c2).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o); }).length; if (n >= 1 && baseOfficers(c2).length >= 3) { var d = hdist(b.c, b.r, c2.c, c2.r); if (d < sd) { sd = d; src = c2; } } });
    if (!src) continue;
    var cand = idleOfficers(src).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o); });
    cand.sort(function (a, c) { return (c.pol - aiMilScore(c) * 0.5) - (a.pol - aiMilScore(a) * 0.5); });
    if (cmdMoveOfficers(src, [cand[0].id], b.id).ok) moved++;
  }
  if ((G.turn + f) % 3 !== 0) return;
  var front = cities.filter(function (c3) { return aiIsFront(f, c3); });
  if (!front.length) return;
  for (ci = 0; ci < cities.length; ci++) {
    b = cities[ci];
    if (front.indexOf(b) >= 0 || F.ap < AP.deploy + 10) continue;
    var dest = null, bd = 1e9;
    front.forEach(function (fb) { var d = hdist(b.c, b.r, fb.c, fb.r) + fb.troops / 3000; if (d < bd) { bd = d; dest = fb; } });
    if (!dest) continue;
    var here = baseOfficers(b).length;
    var idle = idleOfficers(b).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o); });
    // 2) 병력·물자 수송 (태수 한 명은 남긴다)
    if (b.troops >= 6000 && idle.length && here >= 2) {
      idle.sort(function (a, c) { return aiMilScore(a) - aiMilScore(c); });
      var keep = 3000, troops = b.troops - keep;
      var gold = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.gold - 800) * 0.6)), food = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.food - Math.ceil(keep / 60) * 30) * 0.5));
      var cargo = { spear: Math.floor(b.w.spear * 0.6), halberd: Math.floor(b.w.halberd * 0.6), bow: Math.floor(b.w.bow * 0.6), horse: Math.floor(b.w.horse * 0.6), ram: b.w.ram };
      var res = cmdDeploy(b, { type: 'trans', ids: [idle[0].id], troops: troops, food: food, gold: gold, cargo: cargo, toward: dest });
      if (res.ok) { res.unit.ai = { mode: 'supply', target: dest.id, home: b.id }; idle.shift(); here--; }
    } else if (F.ap >= 10 && (b.gold > 2500 || b.food > 30000)) {
      // 3) 후방의 남는 금·병량은 전선으로 보낸다 (AI 전용 간이 수송)
      var g2 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.gold - 1200) * 0.7)), f2 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.food - 15000) * 0.6));
      b.gold -= g2; b.food -= f2; dest.gold += g2; dest.food += f2; F.ap -= 10;
    }
    if (idle.length > 1 && here > 3 && F.ap >= AP.move) {
      idle.sort(function (a, c) { return aiMilScore(c) - aiMilScore(a); });
      cmdMoveOfficers(b, idle.slice(0, Math.min(2, here - 3, idle.length)).map(function (o) { return o.id; }), dest.id);
    }
  }
}

function aiPhase(f) {
  if (!G.factions[f].alive) return;
  aiUnits(f);
  if (G.over || !G.factions[f].alive) return;
  aiDefense(f);
  aiCaptives(f);
  aiInternal(f);
  aiOffense(f);
  aiLogistics(f);
  var fresh = facUnits(f).filter(function (u) { return !u.done; });
  for (var i = 0; i < fresh.length; i++) { if (G.over) return; aiUnitAct(fresh[i]); }
}

=====8<===== src/ui_draw.js
/* ===== 천하도 — 지도 화면(캔버스)·입력·연출·소리 ===== */
var HEXR = 24, HEXW = Math.sqrt(3) * HEXR, ROWH = 1.5 * HEXR;
var WORLD_W = 2766, WORLD_H = 2878;
var FONT_D = '"Noto Serif KR","Nanum Myeongjo","AppleMyungjo","Batang",serif';
var FONT_B = '"Apple SD Gothic Neo","Noto Sans KR","Malgun Gothic",system-ui,sans-serif';
var cv, cx, DPR = 1, CW = 0, CH = 0;
var cam = { x: 1500, y: 1100, z: 1 };
var UI = { sel: null, rc: null, target: null, busy: false, anims: [], floats: [], dirty: true, sound: true, hide: -1, ghost: null, lastDraw: 0 };
var IMG = { map: null, por: {} }, POR_URL = {};

function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
function fmt(n) { return Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US'); }
function requestDraw() { UI.dirty = true; }

function b64blob(b64, type) {
  var bin = atob(b64), n = bin.length, a = new Uint8Array(n);
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) a[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return new Blob([a], { type: type });
}
function loadAssets() {
  for (var k in PORTRAITS) {
    POR_URL[k] = URL.createObjectURL(b64blob(PORTRAITS[k], 'image/webp'));
    var im = new Image(); im.onload = requestDraw; im.src = POR_URL[k]; IMG.por[k] = im;
  }
  IMG.map = new Image(); IMG.map.onload = requestDraw; IMG.map.src = MAP_IMG;
  $('title').style.backgroundImage = 'url(' + TITLE_IMG + ')';
}

/* ---------- 좌표 ---------- */
function hexCenter(c, r) { return [HEXW * (c + 0.5 * (r & 1)) + HEXW / 2, HEXR + r * ROWH]; }
function worldToHex(wx, wy) {
  var r0 = Math.round((wy - HEXR) / ROWH), best = null, bd = 1e9;
  for (var r = r0 - 1; r <= r0 + 1; r++) {
    if (r < 0 || r >= H) continue;
    var c0 = Math.round((wx - HEXW / 2) / HEXW - 0.5 * (r & 1));
    for (var c = c0 - 1; c <= c0 + 1; c++) {
      if (c < 0 || c >= W) continue;
      var p = hexCenter(c, r), d = (p[0] - wx) * (p[0] - wx) + (p[1] - wy) * (p[1] - wy);
      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = [c, r]; }
    }
  }
  return best;
}
function s2w(sx, sy) { return [(sx - CW / 2) / cam.z + cam.x, (sy - CH / 2) / cam.z + cam.y]; }
function w2s(wx, wy) { return [(wx - cam.x) * cam.z + CW / 2, (wy - cam.y) * cam.z + CH / 2]; }
function zMin() { return Math.max(0.13, Math.min(0.5, CW / WORLD_W)); }
function clampCam() {
  cam.z = clamp(cam.z, zMin(), 2.4);
  var vw = CW / cam.z, vh = CH / cam.z;
  if (vw >= WORLD_W) cam.x = WORLD_W / 2; else cam.x = clamp(cam.x, vw * 0.35, WORLD_W - vw * 0.35);
  if (vh >= WORLD_H) cam.y = WORLD_H / 2; else cam.y = clamp(cam.y, vh * 0.3, WORLD_H - vh * 0.05);
}
function resize() {
  DPR = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
  CW = cv.clientWidth; CH = cv.clientHeight;
  cv.width = Math.round(CW * DPR); cv.height = Math.round(CH * DPR);
  clampCam(); requestDraw();
}
function jumpTo(c, r, z, fy) {
  var p = hexCenter(c, r), z1 = z || cam.z, x0 = cam.x, y0 = cam.y, z0 = cam.z;
  var ty = p[1] + (0.5 - (fy || 0.5)) * CH / z1;
  anim(220, function (t) { var e = t * (2 - t); cam.x = x0 + (p[0] - x0) * e; cam.y = y0 + (ty - y0) * e; cam.z = z0 + (z1 - z0) * e; clampCam(); });
}
function ensureVisible(c, r, sheetOpen) {
  var p = hexCenter(c, r), s = w2s(p[0], p[1]);
  var bottom = sheetOpen ? CH * 0.42 : CH - 90;
  if (s[0] < 40 || s[0] > CW - 40 || s[1] < 90 || s[1] > bottom) jumpTo(c, r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.75), sheetOpen ? 0.27 : 0.45);
}

/* ---------- 연출 ---------- */
function anim(dur, step, done) { UI.anims.push({ t0: performance.now(), dur: dur, step: step, done: done }); requestDraw(); }
function floatText(c, r, text, color, delay, big) {
  var p = hexCenter(c, r);
  UI.floats.push({ x: p[0], y: p[1] - 16, text: text, color: color || '#fff', t0: performance.now() + (delay || 0), dur: 1100, big: !!big });
  requestDraw();
}
function flashHex(c, r, color) {
  var p = hexCenter(c, r);
  UI.floats.push({ x: p[0], y: p[1], ring: true, color: color || '#fff', t0: performance.now(), dur: 320 });
  requestDraw();
}
/* 부대 이동 연출: 유령 말이 경로를 따라 움직인다 */
function animMove(vis, from, path, done) {
  var pts = [hexCenter(from[0], from[1])];
  for (var i = 0; i < path.length; i++) { var k = path[i], c = k % W; pts.push(hexCenter(c, (k - c) / W)); }
  UI.busy = true; UI.ghost = { vis: vis, x: pts[0][0], y: pts[0][1] };
  var dur = Math.min(650, 70 * path.length + 60), last = -1;
  anim(dur, function (t) {
    var f = t * (pts.length - 1), i2 = Math.min(pts.length - 2, Math.floor(f)), u = f - i2;
    UI.ghost.x = pts[i2][0] + (pts[i2 + 1][0] - pts[i2][0]) * u; UI.ghost.y = pts[i2][1] + (pts[i2 + 1][1] - pts[i2][1]) * u;
    if (i2 !== last) { last = i2; sfx('step'); }
  }, function () { UI.ghost = null; UI.hide = -1; UI.busy = false; requestDraw(); if (done) done(); });
}
function unitVis(u) { var L = uLeader(u); return { color: G.factions[u.f].color, por: L.por, type: u.type, ratio: u.troops / Math.max(1, u.max), name: L.n }; }

/* ---------- 소리 (잡음 기반, 삑삑거리지 않게) ---------- */
var AC = null, NOISE = null;
function audioInit() {
  if (AC || !UI.sound) return;
  try {
    AC = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    var n = AC.sampleRate, b = AC.createBuffer(1, n, AC.sampleRate), d = b.getChannelData(0);
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    NOISE = b;
  } catch (e) { AC = null; }
}
function nz(t, dur, type, freq, q, vol) {
  var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = NOISE; s.loop = true;
  var f = AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = type; f.frequency.value = freq; f.Q.value = q;
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + dur);
  s.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(AC.destination); s.start(t, Math.random() * 0.5); s.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
}
function thump(t, f0, f1, dur, vol) {
  var o = AC.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f1, t + dur);
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + dur);
  o.connect(g); g.connect(AC.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
}
function sfx(kind) {
  if (!AC || !UI.sound) return;
  try {
    if (AC.state === 'suspended') AC.resume();
    var t = AC.currentTime;
    if (kind === 'step') nz(t, 0.06, 'lowpass', 420, 0.7, 0.10);
    else if (kind === 'ui') nz(t, 0.035, 'bandpass', 1900, 1.2, 0.06);
    else if (kind === 'hit') { nz(t, 0.16, 'bandpass', 1100, 0.9, 0.34); nz(t + 0.03, 0.22, 'lowpass', 300, 0.7, 0.3); thump(t, 120, 48, 0.18, 0.5); }
    else if (kind === 'big') { nz(t, 0.3, 'bandpass', 800, 0.7, 0.45); thump(t, 110, 38, 0.4, 0.8); nz(t + 0.08, 0.5, 'lowpass', 220, 0.6, 0.4); }
    else if (kind === 'fire') { nz(t, 0.7, 'bandpass', 2600, 0.6, 0.16); nz(t, 0.9, 'lowpass', 500, 0.5, 0.18); }
    else if (kind === 'drum') { thump(t, 95, 42, 0.5, 0.8); nz(t, 0.12, 'lowpass', 260, 0.7, 0.25); }
    else if (kind === 'fall') { thump(t, 95, 40, 0.5, 0.8); thump(t + 0.28, 95, 40, 0.6, 0.8); nz(t, 0.9, 'lowpass', 350, 0.6, 0.3); }
    else if (kind === 'fail') nz(t, 0.2, 'lowpass', 240, 0.8, 0.2);
  } catch (e) { /* 소리는 없어도 된다 */ }
}

/* ---------- 그리기 ---------- */
function hexPath(x, y, k) {
  cx.beginPath();
  for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++) { var a = Math.PI / 180 * (60 * i - 30), px = x + HEXR * k * Math.cos(a), py = y + HEXR * k * Math.sin(a); if (i) cx.lineTo(px, py); else cx.moveTo(px, py); }
  cx.closePath();
}
function labelSize(base) { return base * Math.pow(cam.z, 0.3) / cam.z; }
function outlined(text, x, y, px, fill, stroke, font, weight) {
  cx.font = (weight || 700) + ' ' + px + 'px ' + (font || FONT_B);
  cx.lineJoin = 'round'; cx.lineWidth = px * 0.26; cx.strokeStyle = stroke; cx.strokeText(text, x, y);
  cx.fillStyle = fill; cx.fillText(text, x, y);
}
function drawBase(b) {
  var p = hexCenter(b.c, b.r), x = p[0], y = p[1];
  var col = b.owner >= 0 ? G.factions[b.owner].color : '#8f877a';
  var s = b.kind === 'gate' ? 0.9 : (b.sz === 'L' ? 1.2 : b.sz === 'M' ? 1.06 : 0.95);
  s *= Math.max(1, 0.62 / cam.z);
  var gate = b.kind === 'gate', hw = (gate ? 19 : 23) * s, wy = y - 3 * s, wh = 17 * s, i;
  cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,14,8,0.28)'; cx.beginPath(); cx.ellipse(x, y + 15 * s, hw * 1.15, 7 * s, 0, 0, 6.3); cx.fill();
  cx.lineWidth = 1.5 * s; cx.strokeStyle = '#231a12'; cx.lineJoin = 'round';
  // 성벽(이름판)
  cx.fillStyle = gate ? '#cdbf9e' : '#e4d6b2';
  cx.beginPath(); cx.rect(x - hw, wy, hw * 2, wh); cx.fill(); cx.stroke();
  var n = gate ? 4 : 5, cw = hw * 2 / (n * 2 - 1);
  for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { cx.beginPath(); cx.rect(x - hw + i * cw * 2, wy - 4.5 * s, cw, 4.5 * s); cx.fill(); cx.stroke(); }
  // 지붕
  cx.fillStyle = col;
  cx.beginPath(); cx.moveTo(x - hw * 0.92, wy - 4.5 * s); cx.quadraticCurveTo(x - hw * 0.6, wy - 7 * s, x - hw * 0.52, wy - 15 * s); cx.lineTo(x + hw * 0.52, wy - 15 * s); cx.quadraticCurveTo(x + hw * 0.6, wy - 7 * s, x + hw * 0.92, wy - 4.5 * s); cx.closePath(); cx.fill(); cx.stroke();
  if (b.sz === 'L') { cx.beginPath(); cx.moveTo(x - hw * 0.56, wy - 15 * s); cx.quadraticCurveTo(x - hw * 0.36, wy - 17 * s, x - hw * 0.3, wy - 23 * s); cx.lineTo(x + hw * 0.3, wy - 23 * s); cx.quadraticCurveTo(x + hw * 0.36, wy - 17 * s, x + hw * 0.56, wy - 15 * s); cx.closePath(); cx.fill(); cx.stroke(); }
  // 이름
  cx.textAlign = 'center'; cx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  var fs = Math.min(wh * 0.86, hw * 2 / (b.n.length * 1.02));
  cx.font = '900 ' + fs + 'px ' + FONT_D; cx.fillStyle = '#231a12'; cx.fillText(b.n, x, wy + wh * 0.54);
  cx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  if (cam.z >= 0.45 && b.owner >= 0) outlined(fmt(b.troops), x, wy + wh + 10 * s, 9.5 * s, '#231a12', 'rgba(240,226,196,0.94)', FONT_B, 700);
  if (b.owner === G.player && cam.z >= 0.45) {
    var idle = 0; for (i = 0; i < G.officers.length; i++) { var o = G.officers[i]; if (o.st === 'active' && o.f === b.owner && o.loc === b.id && o.unit === null && !o.acted) idle++; }
    if (idle) { var br = 7.5 * s; cx.fillStyle = '#b3392e'; cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x + hw, wy - 13 * s, br, 0, 6.3); cx.fill(); cx.lineWidth = s; cx.strokeStyle = '#fff'; cx.stroke(); cx.fillStyle = '#fff'; cx.textBaseline = 'middle'; cx.font = '700 ' + br * 1.25 + 'px ' + FONT_B; cx.fillText(idle, x + hw, wy - 13 * s + br * 0.08); cx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic'; }
  }
}
function drawToken(x, y, vis, opt) {
  opt = opt || {};
  var rr = Math.max(HEXR * 0.8, 7.5 / cam.z);
  cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,14,8,0.3)'; cx.beginPath(); cx.ellipse(x, y + rr * 0.85, rr * 0.9, rr * 0.3, 0, 0, 6.3); cx.fill();
  cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x, y, rr, 0, 6.3); cx.fillStyle = vis.color; cx.fill();
  var im = IMG.por[vis.por];
  if (cam.z >= 0.45 && im && im.complete && im.naturalWidth) {
    cx.save(); cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x, y, rr - 2.2, 0, 6.3); cx.clip();
    cx.drawImage(im, 26, 2, 92, 92, x - rr, y - rr, rr * 2, rr * 2);
    cx.restore();
  }
  cx.lineWidth = 2.4; cx.strokeStyle = opt.sel ? '#ffd76a' : (opt.done ? '#8b8478' : '#fff'); cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x, y, rr, 0, 6.3); cx.stroke();
  cx.lineWidth = 1; cx.strokeStyle = '#231a12'; cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x, y, rr + 1.6, 0, 6.3); cx.stroke();
  if (opt.done) { cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(24,18,12,0.5)'; cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x, y, rr, 0, 6.3); cx.fill(); }
  // 병과 표식
  var bx = x - rr * 0.74, by = y + rr * 0.6, br = Math.max(8, rr * 0.44);
  cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(bx, by, br, 0, 6.3); cx.fillStyle = '#17120d'; cx.fill(); cx.lineWidth = 1.2; cx.strokeStyle = vis.color; cx.stroke();
  cx.fillStyle = '#fff'; cx.textAlign = 'center'; cx.textBaseline = 'middle'; cx.font = '700 ' + br * 1.25 + 'px ' + FONT_B; cx.fillText(UNIT_TYPES[vis.type].g, bx, by + br * 0.08);
  // 병력 막대
  var bw = rr * 1.7, bh = Math.max(3.5, rr * 0.2), b0 = y + rr + 3;
  cx.fillStyle = '#17120d'; cx.fillRect(x - bw / 2 - 1, b0 - 1, bw + 2, bh + 2);
  cx.fillStyle = vis.ratio > 0.6 ? '#6fb36a' : (vis.ratio > 0.3 ? '#e0a23c' : '#d6513f'); cx.fillRect(x - bw / 2, b0, bw * clamp(vis.ratio, 0.03, 1), bh);
  cx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  if (opt.status) { cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x + rr * 0.74, y - rr * 0.62, br, 0, 6.3); cx.fillStyle = '#6b3fa0'; cx.fill(); cx.fillStyle = '#fff'; cx.textBaseline = 'middle'; cx.fillText(opt.status === 'confuse' ? '혼' : '퇴', x + rr * 0.74, y - rr * 0.62 + br * 0.08); cx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic'; }
  if (opt.label && cam.z >= 0.62) { var fs = labelSize(11); outlined(opt.label, x, b0 + bh + fs * 1.0, fs, '#fff', 'rgba(23,18,13,0.9)', FONT_B, 700); }
}
function drawFac(p, fc, own) {
  var q = hexCenter(p.c, p.r), x = q[0], y = q[1], F = FAC[fc.t];
  hexPath(x, y, 0.84);
  cx.globalAlpha = fc.done ? 0.95 : 0.55; cx.fillStyle = F.color; cx.fill(); cx.globalAlpha = 1;
  cx.lineWidth = 1.6; cx.strokeStyle = '#231a12'; cx.stroke();
  cx.fillStyle = '#fff'; cx.textAlign = 'center'; cx.textBaseline = 'middle'; cx.font = '900 17px ' + FONT_D; cx.fillText(F.g, x, y + 1);
  cx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  if (fc.lv > 1) { for (var i = 0; i < fc.lv; i++) { cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(x - (fc.lv - 1) * 3.5 + i * 7, y - 13, 2.3, 0, 6.3); cx.fillStyle = '#ffd76a'; cx.fill(); cx.lineWidth = 0.8; cx.strokeStyle = '#231a12'; cx.stroke(); } }
  if (!fc.done) { cx.fillStyle = '#17120d'; cx.fillRect(x - 13, y + 10, 26, 4); cx.fillStyle = '#ffd76a'; cx.fillRect(x - 12, y + 11, 24 * clamp(fc.prog / fc.need, 0, 1), 2); }
}
function draw() {
  UI.dirty = false; UI.lastDraw = performance.now();
  var z = cam.z, i, j, k, p;
  cx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  cx.fillStyle = '#3a3024'; cx.fillRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
  cx.setTransform(DPR * z, 0, 0, DPR * z, DPR * (CW / 2 - cam.x * z), DPR * (CH / 2 - cam.y * z));
  var vw = CW / z, vh = CH / z, L = cam.x - vw / 2, T = cam.y - vh / 2, R = L + vw, B = T + vh;
  if (IMG.map && IMG.map.complete && IMG.map.naturalWidth) {
    var sx = Math.max(0, L), sy = Math.max(0, T), ex = Math.min(WORLD_W, R), ey = Math.min(WORLD_H, B);
    if (ex > sx && ey > sy) cx.drawImage(IMG.map, sx, sy, ex - sx, ey - sy, sx, sy, ex - sx, ey - sy);
  }
  cx.lineWidth = 3 / z; cx.strokeStyle = '#c9a45a'; cx.strokeRect(0, 0, WORLD_W, WORLD_H);
  if (!G) return;
  var c0 = clamp(Math.floor(L / HEXW) - 2, 0, W - 1), c1 = clamp(Math.ceil(R / HEXW) + 2, 0, W - 1), r0 = clamp(Math.floor(T / ROWH) - 2, 0, H - 1), r1 = clamp(Math.ceil(B / ROWH) + 2, 0, H - 1);
  function vis(c, r) { return c >= c0 && c <= c1 && r >= r0 && r <= r1; }
  // 격자: 지나갈 수 있는 칸만
  if (z >= 0.5) {
    cx.beginPath();
    for (var gr = r0; gr <= r1; gr++) for (var gc = c0; gc <= c1; gc++) {
      var gt = MAPDATA.t.charCodeAt(gr * W + gc);
      if (gt === 120 || gt === 115 || gt === 109) continue;      // x s m
      var gx = HEXW * (gc + 0.5 * (gr & 1)) + HEXW / 2, gy = HEXR + gr * ROWH;
      cx.moveTo(gx, gy - HEXR); cx.lineTo(gx + HEXW / 2, gy - HEXR / 2); cx.lineTo(gx + HEXW / 2, gy + HEXR / 2); cx.lineTo(gx, gy + HEXR);
      cx.lineTo(gx - HEXW / 2, gy + HEXR / 2); cx.lineTo(gx - HEXW / 2, gy - HEXR / 2); cx.closePath();
    }
    cx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.6, 0.9 / z); cx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(48,34,18,' + (z >= 0.8 ? 0.2 : 0.13) + ')'; cx.stroke();
  }
  // 세력권
  for (i = 0; i < G.bases.length; i++) {
    var b = G.bases[i];
    if (b.owner < 0 || !vis(b.c, b.r)) continue;
    p = hexCenter(b.c, b.r);
    cx.globalAlpha = 0.17; cx.fillStyle = G.factions[b.owner].color;
    cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(p[0], p[1], HEXW * (b.kind === 'gate' ? 1.3 : 2.5), 0, 6.3); cx.fill();
    cx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
  // 부지·시설
  if (z >= 0.4) {
    for (i = 0; i < G.bases.length; i++) {
      var pb = G.bases[i];
      if (pb.kind !== 'city' || pb.c < c0 - 3 || pb.c > c1 + 3 || pb.r < r0 - 3 || pb.r > r1 + 3) continue;
      for (j = 0; j < pb.plots.length; j++) {
        var pl = pb.plots[j];
        if (pl.fac) drawFac(pl, pl.fac, pb.owner === G.player);
        else if (pb.owner === G.player) {
          p = hexCenter(pl.c, pl.r);
          hexPath(p[0], p[1], 0.8); cx.setLineDash([5, 4]); cx.lineWidth = 1.6; cx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,238,190,0.85)'; cx.stroke(); cx.setLineDash([]);
          cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,238,190,0.16)'; cx.fill();
        }
      }
    }
  }
  // 불
  var now = performance.now();
  for (k in G.fires) {
    k = +k; var fc = k % W, fr = (k - fc) / W;
    if (!vis(fc, fr)) continue;
    p = hexCenter(fc, fr);
    hexPath(p[0], p[1], 0.92); cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(214,81,40,0.28)'; cx.fill();
    for (j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
      var ph = now / 130 + j * 1.7 + k, fx = p[0] + (j - 1.5) * 7, fh = 13 + Math.sin(ph) * 4 + (j % 2) * 4;
      cx.beginPath(); cx.moveTo(fx - 5, p[1] + 8); cx.quadraticCurveTo(fx - 6, p[1] - fh * 0.4, fx + Math.sin(ph * 1.3) * 2, p[1] - fh); cx.quadraticCurveTo(fx + 6, p[1] - fh * 0.4, fx + 5, p[1] + 8); cx.closePath();
      cx.fillStyle = j % 2 ? '#f6b13c' : '#e2542b'; cx.globalAlpha = 0.88; cx.fill(); cx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
  }
  // 이동 범위·대상
  var su = selUnit();
  if (su && UI.rc && !UI.busy) {
    for (k in UI.rc.stop) {
      k = +k; var hc = k % W, hr = (k - hc) / W;
      if (!vis(hc, hr)) continue;
      p = hexCenter(hc, hr);
      hexPath(p[0], p[1], 0.9); cx.fillStyle = BAT[k] !== undefined ? 'rgba(255,215,106,0.42)' : 'rgba(64,138,235,0.30)'; cx.fill();
      cx.lineWidth = 1.2; cx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(210,232,255,0.75)'; cx.stroke();
    }
    var ats = UI.atk || [];
    for (i = 0; i < ats.length; i++) { p = hexCenter(ats[i].c, ats[i].r); hexPath(p[0], p[1], 0.96); cx.lineWidth = 3; cx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(226,60,44,0.95)'; cx.stroke(); cx.fillStyle = 'rgba(226,60,44,0.18)'; cx.fill(); }
  }
  if (UI.target) { p = hexCenter(UI.target.c, UI.target.r); hexPath(p[0], p[1], 1.02); cx.lineWidth = 4; cx.strokeStyle = '#ffd76a'; cx.stroke(); }
  if (UI.sel && UI.sel.kind === 'base') { var sb = G.bases[UI.sel.id]; p = hexCenter(sb.c, sb.r); hexPath(p[0], p[1], 1.25); cx.lineWidth = 3; cx.strokeStyle = '#ffd76a'; cx.setLineDash([8, 5]); cx.stroke(); cx.setLineDash([]); }
  // 거점
  for (i = 0; i < G.bases.length; i++) { if (vis(G.bases[i].c, G.bases[i].r)) drawBase(G.bases[i]); }
  // 부대
  for (i = 0; i < G.units.length; i++) {
    var u = G.units[i];
    if (!vis(u.c, u.r) || u.id === UI.hide) continue;
    p = hexCenter(u.c, u.r);
    var mine = u.f === G.player;
    drawToken(p[0], p[1], unitVis(u), { sel: UI.sel && UI.sel.kind === 'unit' && UI.sel.id === u.id, done: mine && u.done, status: u.status, label: (cam.z >= 1.25 ? uLeader(u).n + ' ' : '') + fmt(u.troops) });
  }
  if (UI.ghost) drawToken(UI.ghost.x, UI.ghost.y, UI.ghost.vis, {});
  // 떠오르는 글자
  for (i = UI.floats.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var f = UI.floats[i], t = (now - f.t0) / f.dur;
    if (t < 0) continue;
    if (t >= 1) { UI.floats.splice(i, 1); continue; }
    if (f.ring) { cx.globalAlpha = 1 - t; cx.lineWidth = 5 * (1 - t) + 1; cx.strokeStyle = f.color; cx.beginPath(); cx.arc(f.x, f.y, HEXR * (0.5 + t * 1.1), 0, 6.3); cx.stroke(); cx.globalAlpha = 1; continue; }
    var fs = labelSize(f.big ? 19 : 16);
    cx.globalAlpha = t < 0.7 ? 1 : (1 - t) / 0.3; cx.textAlign = 'center';
    outlined(f.text, f.x, f.y - t * 34 / Math.sqrt(z), fs, f.color, 'rgba(23,18,13,0.95)', FONT_D, 900);
    cx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }
}
function tick(now) {
  for (var i = UI.anims.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var a = UI.anims[i], t = clamp((now - a.t0) / a.dur, 0, 1);
    a.step(t);
    if (t >= 1) { UI.anims.splice(i, 1); if (a.done) a.done(); }
    UI.dirty = true;
  }
  if (UI.floats.length) UI.dirty = true;
  if (G && now - UI.lastDraw > 110) { for (var k in G.fires) { UI.dirty = true; break; } }
  if (UI.dirty && cx) draw();
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

/* ---------- 입력 ---------- */
var PT = {}, pinch = null, tapOK = false;
function ptCount() { var n = 0; for (var k in PT) n++; return n; }
function ptList() { var a = []; for (var k in PT) a.push(PT[k]); return a; }
function initInput() {
  cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) {
    audioInit();
    try { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (er) { /* */ }
    PT[e.pointerId] = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, t: performance.now(), moved: false };
    if (ptCount() === 2) {
      var a = ptList(), dx = a[0].x - a[1].x, dy = a[0].y - a[1].y, rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
      var mx = (a[0].x + a[1].x) / 2 - rect.left, my = (a[0].y + a[1].y) / 2 - rect.top;
      pinch = { d: Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1, z: cam.z, w: s2w(mx, my) };
      a[0].moved = a[1].moved = true;
    }
  });
  cv.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
    var p = PT[e.pointerId]; if (!p) return;
    var dx = e.clientX - p.x, dy = e.clientY - p.y;
    p.x = e.clientX; p.y = e.clientY;
    if (ptCount() >= 2 && pinch) {
      var a = ptList(), d = Math.hypot(a[0].x - a[1].x, a[0].y - a[1].y) || 1, rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
      var mx = (a[0].x + a[1].x) / 2 - rect.left, my = (a[0].y + a[1].y) / 2 - rect.top;
      cam.z = clamp(pinch.z * d / pinch.d, zMin(), 2.4);
      cam.x = pinch.w[0] - (mx - CW / 2) / cam.z; cam.y = pinch.w[1] - (my - CH / 2) / cam.z;
      clampCam(); requestDraw();
      return;
    }
    if (!p.moved && Math.hypot(e.clientX - p.sx, e.clientY - p.sy) > 7) p.moved = true;
    if (p.moved) { cam.x -= dx / cam.z; cam.y -= dy / cam.z; clampCam(); requestDraw(); }
  });
  function up(e) {
    var p = PT[e.pointerId]; if (!p) return;
    delete PT[e.pointerId];
    if (ptCount() < 2) pinch = null;
    tapOK = (e.type === 'pointerup' && !p.moved && performance.now() - p.t < 700 && ptCount() === 0);
  }
  cv.addEventListener('pointerup', up); cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
  // 탭은 click 에서 처리한다(뒤따라오는 유령 클릭이 새로 뜬 창을 누르지 않게)
  cv.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    if (!tapOK) return;
    tapOK = false;
    var rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect(), w = s2w(e.clientX - rect.left, e.clientY - rect.top), h = worldToHex(w[0], w[1]);
    if (h) onTap(h[0], h[1]);
  });
  cv.addEventListener('wheel', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect(), mx = e.clientX - rect.left, my = e.clientY - rect.top, w = s2w(mx, my);
    cam.z = clamp(cam.z * (e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.15 : 1 / 1.15), zMin(), 2.4);
    cam.x = w[0] - (mx - CW / 2) / cam.z; cam.y = w[1] - (my - CH / 2) / cam.z;
    clampCam(); requestDraw();
  }, { passive: false });
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
}

=====8<===== src/ui_panels.js
/* ===== 천하도 — 패널·대화상자·조작 흐름 ===== */
var SHEET = null, MODAL = null, toastT = 0;
var SIZE_NAME = { L: '대도시', M: '중도시', S: '소도시', G: '관문' };

function toast(msg, ms) {
  var t = $('toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add('on');
  clearTimeout(toastT); toastT = setTimeout(function () { t.classList.remove('on'); }, ms || 2400);
}
function showSheet(s) { SHEET = s; var el = $('sheet'); el.className = 'on' + (s.cls ? ' ' + s.cls : ''); el.innerHTML = s.render(); }
function refreshSheet() {
  if (!SHEET) return;
  var el = $('sheet'), body = el.querySelector('.sh-body'), st = body ? body.scrollTop : 0;
  el.innerHTML = SHEET.render();
  body = el.querySelector('.sh-body'); if (body) body.scrollTop = st;
}
function closeSheet() { SHEET = null; var el = $('sheet'); el.className = ''; el.innerHTML = ''; }
function showDialog(d) { MODAL = d; var m = $('modal'); m.innerHTML = '<div class="dlg">' + d.render() + '</div>'; m.classList.add('on'); }
function refreshDialog() {
  if (!MODAL) return;
  var m = $('modal'), b = m.querySelector('.dlg-b'), st = b ? b.scrollTop : 0;
  m.innerHTML = '<div class="dlg">' + MODAL.render() + '</div>';
  b = m.querySelector('.dlg-b'); if (b) b.scrollTop = st;
}
function closeDialog() { MODAL = null; var m = $('modal'); m.classList.remove('on'); m.innerHTML = ''; }
function dlgHead(title) { return '<div class="dlg-h"><h2>' + title + '</h2><button class="x" data-a="close" type="button" aria-label="닫기">×</button></div>'; }
function delegate(root, getH, onClose) {
  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var el = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-a]') : null;
    if (!el || !root.contains(el)) { if (root.id === 'modal' && e.target === root) closeDialog(); return; }
    var a = el.getAttribute('data-a'), hs = getH();
    audioInit(); sfx('ui');
    if (a === 'close') { onClose(); return; }
    if (hs && hs.on && hs.on[a]) hs.on[a](el);
  });
  root.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var el = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-i]') : null; if (!el) return;
    var hs = getH(), a = el.getAttribute('data-i');
    if (hs && hs.on && hs.on[a]) hs.on[a](el);
  });
}

/* ---------- 공통 조각 ---------- */
function porTag(o, cls) { return '<img class="por' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" alt="" src="' + (POR_URL[o.por] || '') + '">'; }
function st5(o, hi) {
  var ks = [['ldr', '통'], ['war', '무'], ['int', '지'], ['pol', '정'], ['cha', '매']], s = '<span class="st5">';
  for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) s += '<span><i>' + ks[i][1] + '</i> <b' + (hi === ks[i][0] ? ' class="hi"' : '') + '>' + o[ks[i][0]] + '</b></span>';
  return s + '</span>';
}
function skTag(o) { return o.sk ? '<em>' + o.sk + '</em>' : ''; }
function facChip(f) { return '<span class="chip" style="background:' + (f >= 0 ? G.factions[f].color : '#7d766a') + '">' + (f >= 0 ? G.factions[f].name : '빈 땅') + '</span>'; }
function offStatus(o) {
  if (o.st === 'travel') return '이동 중 (' + o.eta + '턴)';
  if (o.unit !== null) return '출진 중';
  return o.acted ? '행동 완료' : '대기';
}
function offRow(o, opt) {
  opt = opt || {};
  return '<button class="row' + (opt.sel ? ' sel' : '') + (opt.dim ? ' dim' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="' + (opt.a || 'off') + '" data-v="' + o.id + '">' + porTag(o) +
    '<span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + (opt.badge ? '<span class="hi">' + opt.badge + '</span> ' : '') + o.n + skTag(o) + '</span><span class="ds">' + (opt.ds !== undefined ? opt.ds : st5(o, opt.hi)) + '</span></span>' +
    (opt.rt ? '<span class="rt">' + opt.rt + '</span>' : '') + '</button>';
}

/* 무장 선택. opts: {title, list, min, max, hi, key(o), rt(o), preview(os), ok} */
function pickOfficers(opts, cb) {
  var sel = [], list = opts.list.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return opts.key(b) - opts.key(a); });
  if (!list.length) { toast('행동할 수 있는 무장이 없습니다'); return; }
  var max = opts.max || 1, min = opts.min || 1;
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var h = dlgHead(opts.title) + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="rows">';
      for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        var o = list[i], si = sel.indexOf(o.id);
        h += offRow(o, { a: 'pick', sel: si >= 0, hi: opts.hi, rt: opts.rt ? opts.rt(o) : '', badge: si >= 0 && max > 1 ? (si + 1) : '' });
      }
      var os = sel.map(off);
      h += '</div></div><div class="dlg-f"><div class="pv">' + (os.length ? (opts.preview ? opts.preview(os) : os.length + '명 선택') : (max > 1 ? '무장을 고르세요 (최대 ' + max + '명)' : '무장을 고르세요')) + '</div>' +
        '<button class="btn pri" type="button" data-a="ok"' + (os.length < min ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (opts.ok || '실행') + '</button></div>';
      return h;
    },
    on: {
      pick: function (el) {
        var id = +el.getAttribute('data-v'), i = sel.indexOf(id);
        if (i >= 0) sel.splice(i, 1); else if (max === 1) sel = [id]; else if (sel.length < max) sel.push(id); else { toast('최대 ' + max + '명입니다'); return; }
        refreshDialog();
      },
      ok: function () { if (sel.length < min) return; var os = sel.map(off); closeDialog(); cb(os); }
    }
  });
}
function ids(os) { return os.map(function (o) { return o.id; }); }

/* 무장 상세 */
function showOfficer(o) {
  var apt = ['창', '극', '노', '기', '병기'], a = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) a += '<div class="' + o.apt.charAt(i) + '">' + apt[i] + '<b>' + o.apt.charAt(i) + '</b></div>';
  var bars = '', ks = [['ldr', '통솔'], ['war', '무력'], ['int', '지력'], ['pol', '정치'], ['cha', '매력']];
  for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) bars += '<div class="bar"><span>' + ks[i][1] + '</span><i><u style="width:' + o[ks[i][0]] + '%"></u></i><b>' + o[ks[i][0]] + '</b></div>';
  var where = o.st === 'free' ? '재야 (' + G.bases[o.loc].n + ')' : o.st === 'captive' ? '포로 (' + G.bases[o.loc].n + ')' : (o.unit !== null ? '출진 중' : G.bases[o.loc].n);
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      return dlgHead(o.n + ' ' + (o.f >= 0 ? facChip(o.f) : '')) + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="card">' + porTag(o, 'lg') + '<div class="bars">' + bars + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="apt">' + a + '</div>' +
        (o.sk ? '<p><b class="hi">특기 · ' + o.sk + '</b><br>' + SKILLS[o.sk] + '</p>' : '<p class="note">특기 없음</p>') +
        '<div class="stats"><div><span>소재</span><b>' + where + '</b></div>' + (o.f >= 0 ? '<div><span>충성</span><b>' + o.loy + '</b></div><div><span>지휘</span><b>' + fmt(cmdCap(o)) + '</b></div><div><span>공적</span><b>' + fmt(o.merit) + '</b></div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    }, on: {}
  });
}

/* ---------- HUD ---------- */
function refreshHud() {
  if (!G) return;
  var F = G.factions[G.player];
  $('hudDate').textContent = dateStr();
  $('hudFac').firstElementChild.style.background = F.color;
  $('hudFac').lastElementChild.textContent = F.name;
  $('hudAP').querySelector('b').textContent = F.ap;
  var g = 0, fd = 0, t = 0;
  facBases(G.player).forEach(function (b) { g += b.gold; fd += b.food; t += b.troops; });
  facUnits(G.player).forEach(function (u) { t += u.troops; });
  $('hudRes').innerHTML = '<span>금 <b>' + fmt(g) + '</b></span><span>병량 <b>' + fmt(fd) + '</b></span><span>병력 <b>' + fmt(t) + '</b></span><span>도시 <b>' + facCities(G.player).length + '</b></span>';
  var n = facUnits(G.player).filter(function (u) { return !u.done; }).length;
  $('btnNext').querySelector('.n').textContent = n ? n : '';
  $('btnNext').style.display = facUnits(G.player).length ? '' : 'none';
}
function afterAction() { requestDraw(); refreshHud(); if (G.over) showGameOver(); }

/* ---------- 선택 ---------- */
function selUnit() {
  if (!G || !UI.sel || UI.sel.kind !== 'unit') return null;
  var u = UMAP[UI.sel.id];
  return (u && u.f === G.player && !u.done) ? u : null;
}
function clearSel() { UI.sel = null; UI.rc = null; UI.atk = null; UI.target = null; closeSheet(); requestDraw(); }
function selectBase(b) {
  UI.sel = { kind: 'base', id: b.id }; UI.rc = null; UI.atk = null; UI.target = null;
  showSheet(baseSheet(b)); ensureVisible(b.c, b.r, true); requestDraw();
}
function selectUnit(u) {
  UI.sel = { kind: 'unit', id: u.id }; UI.target = null;
  if (u.f === G.player && !u.done) { UI.rc = unitReach(u); UI.atk = allTargets(u); } else { UI.rc = null; UI.atk = null; }
  showSheet(unitSheet(u)); ensureVisible(u.c, u.r, true); requestDraw();
}
/* 공격·계략이 닿는 대상 전부 */
function allTargets(u) {
  var out = attackTargets(u), seen = {};
  out.forEach(function (t) { seen[K(t.c, t.r)] = 1; });
  for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) ployTargets(u, PLOYS[i]).forEach(function (t) { var k = K(t.c, t.r); if (!seen[k]) { seen[k] = 1; out.push(t.u ? { kind: 'unit', u: t.u, c: t.c, r: t.r } : { kind: 'fac', b: plotAt(t.c, t.r).base, i: plotAt(t.c, t.r).i, c: t.c, r: t.r }); } });
  return out;
}
function onTap(c, r) {
  if (UI.busy || !G || G.over || MODAL) return;
  var k = K(c, r), u = UAT[k] || null, b = baseAt(c, r), pl = plotAt(c, r), su = selUnit();
  if (su) {
    if (u === su) return;
    var t = null;
    if (u && u.f !== G.player) t = { kind: 'unit', u: u, c: c, r: r };
    else if (b && b.owner >= 0 && b.owner !== G.player) t = { kind: 'base', b: b, c: c, r: r };
    else if (pl && pl.plot.fac && pl.base.owner >= 0 && pl.base.owner !== G.player) t = { kind: 'fac', b: pl.base, i: pl.i, c: c, r: r };
    if (t) {
      var acts = targetActions(su, t), any = acts.some(function (a) { return a.ok; });
      if (!any) { toast('닿지 않습니다 — 먼저 가까이 이동하세요'); return; }
      UI.target = t; refreshSheet(); requestDraw(); return;
    }
    if (UI.rc && UI.rc.stop[k]) { doMove(su, c, r); return; }
  }
  UI.target = null;
  if (u) { selectUnit(u); return; }
  if (b) { selectBase(b); return; }
  if (pl && pl.base.owner === G.player) { clearSel(); if (pl.plot.fac) flowFacility(pl.base, pl.i); else flowDevelop(pl.base, pl.i); return; }
  if (su || UI.sel) { clearSel(); return; }
  var tt = terr(c, r);
  toast(TERRAIN_NAME[tt] + (isRoad(c, r) && tt !== 'w' ? ' (길)' : (isRoad(c, r) ? ' (나루)' : '')) + ' — ' + TERRAIN_DESC[tt], 1800);
}

/* ---------- 부대 조작 ---------- */
function doMove(u, c, r) {
  var vis = unitVis(u), rc = UI.rc;
  var res = unitMove(u, c, r, rc);
  if (!res.ok) { toast(res.msg); return; }
  UI.hide = alive(u) ? u.id : -1; UI.rc = null; UI.atk = null; UI.target = null;
  animMove(vis, res.from, res.path, function () {
    if (res.entered !== undefined) { toast(G.bases[res.entered].n + ' 입성'); selectBase(G.bases[res.entered]); }
    else if (res.captured !== undefined) { sfx('fall'); toast(G.bases[res.captured].n + ' 점령!'); selectBase(G.bases[res.captured]); }
    else if (res.dead) { toast('불길 속에서 부대가 흩어졌습니다'); clearSel(); }
    else {
      if (res.burn) { floatText(c, r, '-' + fmt(res.burn), '#ff9a5a'); sfx('fire'); }
      UI.rc = unitReach(u); UI.atk = allTargets(u); refreshSheet();
    }
    afterAction();
  });
}
function estBase(u, b) {
  var T = UNIT_TYPES[u.type];
  return { t: Math.min(b.troops, Math.round(620 * Math.pow(uAtk(u) / baseDefVal(b), 0.9) * troopFactor(u.troops) * T.vsBase)), d: durDmgUB(u) };
}
function targetActions(u, t) {
  var out = [], d = hdist(u.c, u.r, t.c, t.r), rg = uRange(u), i;
  if (t.kind === 'unit') {
    out.push({ kind: 'attack', label: '공격', sub: d <= rg ? '예상 -' + fmt(estUU(u, t.u, 1, d > 1)) : '사거리 밖', ok: d <= rg, pri: true });
    var all = TACTICS[u.type] || [], can = tacticList(u);
    for (i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
      var tac = all[i], have = can.indexOf(tac) >= 0, inT = have && tacticTargets(u, tac).some(function (x) { return x.u === t.u; });
      var crit = tacticCrit(u, t.u), rate = Math.round(TACTIC_RATE[uApt(u)] + clamp((uWar(u) - 70) / 3, -5, 8));
      var sub = !have ? '적성 ' + tac.need + ' 필요' : (u.morale < tac.cost ? '기력 부족' : (!inT ? (d > rg ? '사거리 밖' : '숲에서는 불가') : '기력 ' + tac.cost + ' · ' + (crit ? '필중' : rate + '%')));
      out.push({ kind: 'tactic', tac: tac, label: tac.name, sub: sub, ok: inT });
    }
    for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      var pl = PLOYS[i], inP = ployTargets(u, pl).some(function (x) { return x.u === t.u; });
      out.push({ kind: 'ploy', ploy: pl, label: pl.name, sub: inP ? '기력 ' + pl.cost + ' · ' + ployChance(u, pl, t.u) + '%' : (u.morale < pl.cost ? '기력 부족' : (t.u.status && i > 0 ? '이미 걸림' : '사거리 밖')), ok: inP });
    }
  } else if (t.kind === 'base') {
    var e = estBase(u, t.b);
    out.push({ kind: 'attack', label: '공격', sub: d <= rg ? '병력 -' + fmt(e.t) + ' · 내구 -' + fmt(e.d) : '사거리 밖', ok: d <= rg, pri: true });
  } else {
    out.push({ kind: 'attack', label: '시설 파괴', sub: d <= 1 ? '공격' : '옆 칸에서만', ok: d <= 1, pri: true });
    var inF = ployTargets(u, PLOYS[0]).some(function (x) { return x.c === t.c && x.r === t.r; });
    out.push({ kind: 'ploy', ploy: PLOYS[0], label: '화계', sub: inF ? '기력 10' : '불가', ok: inF });
  }
  return out;
}
function presentResult(name, res) {
  var msg = name + ' ', tc = res.tc !== undefined ? res.tc : res.c, tr = res.tr !== undefined ? res.tr : res.r;
  var ac = res.uc !== undefined ? res.uc : res.ac, ar = res.ur !== undefined ? res.ur : res.ar;
  flashHex(tc, tr, '#fff');
  if (res.kind === 'ploy') {
    if (res.success) {
      floatText(res.c, res.r, res.ploy + '!', res.ploy === '화계' ? '#ff9a3c' : '#c9a0ff', 0, true);
      if (res.dmg) floatText(res.c, res.r, '-' + fmt(res.dmg), '#ff6a55', 260);
      sfx(res.ploy === '화계' || res.ploy === '소화' ? 'fire' : 'hit');
      msg += res.ploy + ' 성공' + (res.dmg ? ' (-' + fmt(res.dmg) + ')' : '') + (res.chain ? ' · 연환!' : '');
    } else {
      floatText(res.c, res.r, '실패', '#cfc6b4'); sfx('fail');
      msg += res.ploy + ' 실패 (성공률 ' + res.p + '%)' + (res.reflected ? ' — 반계에 당했습니다!' : '');
      if (res.reflected) floatText(res.ac, res.ar, '반계!', '#c9a0ff', 300, true);
    }
  } else {
    if (res.kind === 'tactic') {
      floatText(res.c, res.r, res.fail ? '전법 실패' : res.tac + (res.crit ? ' 크리티컬!' : '!'), res.fail ? '#cfc6b4' : '#ffd76a', 0, true);
      msg += res.tac + (res.fail ? ' 실패' : (res.crit ? ' 크리티컬' : ' 성공'));
    } else { if (res.crit) floatText(res.c, res.r, '크리티컬!', '#ffd76a', 0, true); msg += '공격'; }
    floatText(tc, tr, '-' + fmt(res.dmg), '#ff6a55', res.kind === 'tactic' || res.crit ? 240 : 0, res.crit);
    msg += ' → ' + res.tname + ' -' + fmt(res.dmg);
    if (res.dur) msg += ' · 내구 -' + fmt(res.dur);
    if (res.dmg2) { floatText(tc, tr, '연전 -' + fmt(res.dmg2), '#ff6a55', 520); msg += ' · 연전 -' + fmt(res.dmg2); }
    if (res.extra) res.extra.forEach(function (x, i) { floatText(x.c, x.r, '-' + fmt(x.dmg), '#ff6a55', 300 + i * 80); flashHex(x.c, x.r, '#fff'); });
    if (res.slam) msg += ' · 뒤가 막혀 추가 피해';
    if (res.confused) { floatText(tc, tr, '혼란', '#c9a0ff', 520); msg += ' · 혼란'; }
    if (res.fire) sfx('fire');
    sfx(res.fail ? 'fail' : (res.kind === 'tactic' || res.crit ? 'big' : 'hit'));
  }
  if (res.counter) { floatText(ac, ar, '-' + fmt(res.counter), '#ffb65a', 420); msg += ' (반격 -' + fmt(res.counter) + ')'; }
  if (res.duel) { floatText(res.duel.win ? tc : ac, res.duel.win ? tr : ar, '일기토 패 -' + fmt(res.duel.loss), '#ffd76a', 700, true); msg += ' · 일기토! ' + res.duel.a + ' 대 ' + res.duel.d + ' — ' + (res.duel.win ? res.duel.a : res.duel.d) + ' 승'; sfx('big'); }
  if (res.selfDmg) floatText(res.ac, res.ar, '-' + fmt(res.selfDmg), '#ffb65a', 500);
  if (res.killed) msg += ' · 궤멸!';
  if (res.destroyed) msg += ' · 파괴!';
  if (res.selfDead) msg += ' · 아군 부대 궤멸…';
  if (res.fell) { msg = res.tname + ' 함락!'; sfx('fall'); }
  toast(msg, 3200);
}
function doAct(a) {
  var u = selUnit(), t = UI.target;
  if (!u || !t || UI.busy) return;
  var name = uName(u), res;
  if (a.kind === 'attack') res = unitAttack(u, t);
  else if (a.kind === 'tactic') res = unitTactic(u, a.tac, t);
  else res = unitPloy(u, a.ploy, t);
  if (!res.ok) { toast(res.msg); return; }
  presentResult(name, res);
  var fell = res.fell && t.kind === 'base' && t.b.owner === G.player ? t.b : null;
  clearSel();
  if (fell) selectBase(fell);
  afterAction();
}
function unitSheet(u) {
  var st = { acts: [] };
  return {
    cls: 'unit',
    on: {
      wait: function () { var x = UMAP[u.id]; if (x) unitWait(x); clearSel(); afterAction(); },
      undo: function () { var x = UMAP[u.id]; if (x && unitUndoMove(x)) { UI.rc = unitReach(x); UI.atk = allTargets(x); UI.target = null; refreshSheet(); requestDraw(); } },
      douse: function () { var x = selUnit(); if (!x) return; var res = unitPloy(x, PLOYS[3], { c: x.c, r: x.r }); if (!res.ok) { toast(res.msg); return; } sfx('fire'); toast('주위의 불을 껐습니다'); clearSel(); afterAction(); },
      act: function (el) { var a = st.acts[+el.getAttribute('data-v')]; if (a && a.ok) doAct(a); else if (a) toast(a.sub); },
      untarget: function () { UI.target = null; refreshSheet(); requestDraw(); },
      off: function (el) { showOfficer(off(+el.getAttribute('data-v'))); }
    },
    render: function () {
      var x = UMAP[u.id];
      if (!x) return '';
      var T = UNIT_TYPES[x.type], mine = x.f === G.player, ms = uMembers(x), i;
      var turns = x.troops > 0 ? Math.floor(x.food / Math.ceil(x.troops / (uHas(x, '둔전') ? 40 : 20))) : 0;
      var h = '<div class="sh-head">' + porTag(ms[0]) + '<div><div class="sh-title">' + uName(x) + ' ' + facChip(x.f) + '</div><div class="sh-sub">' + T.name + (APT_IDX[x.type] !== undefined ? ' 적성 ' + uApt(x) : '') +
        ' · 병력 <b>' + fmt(x.troops) + '</b> · 기력 <b>' + x.morale + '</b> · 군량 ' + fmt(x.food) + ' (' + turns + '턴)' + (x.status ? ' · <b class="bad">' + (x.status === 'confuse' ? '혼란' : '위보') + '</b>' : '') + '</div></div>' +
        '<button class="x" data-a="close" type="button" aria-label="닫기">×</button></div><div class="sh-body">';
      var su = selUnit();
      if (su === x) {
        if (UI.target) {
          var t = UI.target;
          var tn = t.kind === 'unit' ? uName(t.u) + ' · ' + UNIT_TYPES[t.u.type].name + ' ' + fmt(t.u.troops) + '명 · 기력 ' + t.u.morale : (t.kind === 'base' ? t.b.n + ' · 병력 ' + fmt(t.b.troops) + ' · 내구 ' + fmt(t.b.dur) : t.b.n + '의 ' + FAC[t.b.plots[t.i].fac.t].name);
          st.acts = targetActions(x, t);
          h += '<div class="tgt">대상 — <b>' + tn + '</b></div><div class="acts">';
          for (i = 0; i < st.acts.length; i++) { var a = st.acts[i]; h += '<button class="act' + (a.pri && a.ok ? ' pri' : '') + (a.ok ? '' : ' off') + '" type="button" data-a="act" data-v="' + i + '"><b>' + a.label + '</b><small>' + a.sub + '</small></button>'; }
          h += '<button class="act" type="button" data-a="untarget"><b>대상 취소</b><small>&nbsp;</small></button></div>';
        } else {
          var canDouse = ployTargets(x, PLOYS[3]).length > 0;
          h += '<div class="acts"><button class="act" type="button" data-a="wait"><b>대기</b><small>이번 턴 마침</small></button>' +
            '<button class="act' + (x.ox >= 0 ? '' : ' off') + '" type="button" data-a="undo"><b>이동 취소</b><small>' + (x.ox >= 0 ? '되돌리기' : '&nbsp;') + '</small></button>' +
            (canDouse ? '<button class="act" type="button" data-a="douse"><b>소화</b><small>기력 10</small></button>' : '') + '</div>' +
            '<p class="note">파란 칸을 누르면 이동합니다. 적을 누르면 공격·전법·계략을 고릅니다. 자기 거점으로 들어가면 입성합니다.</p>';
        }
      } else if (mine && x.done) h += '<p class="note">이번 턴 행동을 마쳤습니다.</p>';
      h += '<div class="rows">';
      for (i = 0; i < ms.length; i++) h += offRow(ms[i], { badge: i === 0 ? '주장' : '부장' });
      h += '</div></div>';
      return h;
    }
  };
}

/* ---------- 거점 패널 ---------- */
function baseSheet(b0) {
  var st = { tab: b0.kind === 'gate' ? 'mil' : 'home' };
  function B() { return G.bases[b0.id]; }
  var on = {
    tab: function (el) { st.tab = el.getAttribute('data-v'); refreshSheet(); },
    off: function (el) { showOfficer(off(+el.getAttribute('data-v'))); },
    draft: function () { flowDraft(B()); }, patrol: function () { flowPatrol(B()); }, train: function () { flowTrain(B()); },
    produce: function () { flowProduce(B()); }, trade: function () { flowTrade(B()); }, deploy: function () { flowDeploy(B()); },
    trans: function () { flowTransport(B()); }, search: function () { flowSearch(B()); }, recruit: function () { flowRecruit(B()); },
    reward: function () { flowReward(B()); }, move: function () { flowMove(B()); }, captive: function () { flowCaptive(B()); },
    plot: function (el) { var i = +el.getAttribute('data-v'), b = B(); if (b.plots[i].fac) flowFacility(b, i); else flowDevelop(b, i); }
  };
  function cmd(a, name, sub, pri, offc) { return '<button class="cmd' + (pri ? ' pri' : '') + (offc ? ' off' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="' + a + '"><b>' + name + '</b><small>' + sub + '</small></button>'; }
  return {
    on: on,
    render: function () {
      var b = B(), mine = b.owner === G.player, city = b.kind === 'city', i;
      var h = '<div class="sh-head"><div><div class="sh-title">' + b.n + ' ' + facChip(b.owner) + '</div><div class="sh-sub">' + SIZE_NAME[b.sz] + (mine ? ' · 행동 가능 무장 ' + idleOfficers(b).length + '명' : '') + '</div></div><button class="x" data-a="close" type="button" aria-label="닫기">×</button></div><div class="sh-body">';
      h += '<div class="stats">' +
        (city ? '<div><span>금</span><b>' + fmt(b.gold) + (b.owner >= 0 ? ' <small>+' + fmt(goldIncome(b)) + '</small>' : '') + '</b></div><div><span>병량</span><b>' + fmt(b.food) + (b.owner >= 0 ? ' <small>+' + fmt(foodIncome(b)) + '</small>' : '') + '</b></div>' : '<div><span>금</span><b>' + fmt(b.gold) + '</b></div><div><span>병량</span><b>' + fmt(b.food) + '</b></div>') +
        '<div><span>병력</span><b>' + fmt(b.troops) + '</b></div><div><span>기력</span><b>' + b.morale + '</b></div>' + (city ? '<div><span>치안</span><b' + (b.order < 50 ? ' class="bad"' : '') + '>' + b.order + '</b></div>' : '<div><span>&nbsp;</span><b></b></div>') +
        '<div><span>내구</span><b>' + fmt(b.dur) + '/' + fmt(b.maxDur) + '</b></div></div>';
      h += '<div class="wp"><span>창 <b>' + fmt(b.w.spear) + '</b></span><span>극 <b>' + fmt(b.w.halberd) + '</b></span><span>노 <b>' + fmt(b.w.bow) + '</b></span><span>군마 <b>' + fmt(b.w.horse) + '</b></span><span>충차 <b>' + b.w.ram + '</b></span></div>';
      if (!mine) {
        var os = baseOfficers(b);
        h += '<div class="sec">' + (b.owner >= 0 ? '주둔 무장 ' + os.length + '명' : '주인 없는 거점 — 부대를 들여보내면 점령합니다') + '</div><div class="rows">';
        os.sort(function (x, y) { return (isLord(y) ? 1000 : 0) + aiMilScore(y) - (isLord(x) ? 1000 : 0) - aiMilScore(x); });
        for (i = 0; i < os.length; i++) h += offRow(os[i], { badge: isLord(os[i]) ? '군주' : '' });
        var fr0 = freeOfficers(b); for (i = 0; i < fr0.length; i++) h += offRow(fr0[i], { badge: '재야' });
        return h + '</div></div>';
      }
      var tabs = city ? [['home', '내정'], ['mil', '군사'], ['men', '인재'], ['fac', '시설']] : [['mil', '군사'], ['men', '인재']];
      h += '<div class="tabs">'; for (i = 0; i < tabs.length; i++) h += '<button type="button" data-a="tab" data-v="' + tabs[i][0] + '"' + (st.tab === tabs[i][0] ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>' + tabs[i][1] + '</button>'; h += '</div>';
      if (st.tab === 'home') {
        h += '<div class="cmds">' + cmd('draft', '징병', '금 300 · 행 ' + AP.draft, false, !hasFac(b, 'barracks')) + cmd('patrol', '순찰', '금 100 · 행 ' + AP.patrol) + cmd('train', '훈련', '행 ' + AP.train) +
          cmd('produce', '생산', '금 300~ · 행 ' + AP.produce, false, !hasFac(b, 'smithy') && !hasFac(b, 'stable') && !hasFac(b, 'workshop')) + cmd('trade', '거래', '행 ' + AP.trade, false, !hasFac(b, 'market')) + '</div>' +
          '<p class="note">금은 매월 초, 병량은 1·4·7·10월 초에 들어옵니다. 치안이 낮으면 수입과 징병량이 줄어듭니다.</p>';
      } else if (st.tab === 'mil') {
        h += '<div class="cmds">' + cmd('deploy', '출진', '행 ' + AP.deploy, true) + cmd('trans', '수송', '행 ' + AP.deploy) + (city ? '' : cmd('train', '훈련', '행 ' + AP.train)) + '</div>';
        var mu = facUnits(G.player).filter(function (u) { return u.home === b.id; });
        if (mu.length) h += '<p class="note">이 거점에서 나간 부대 ' + mu.length + '개</p>';
      } else if (st.tab === 'men') {
        var fr = freeOfficers(b), cp = b.captives.length;
        h += '<div class="cmds">' + (city ? cmd('search', '탐색', '행 ' + AP.search) : '') + cmd('recruit', '등용', (fr.length || cp) ? '재야 ' + fr.length + ' · 포로 ' + cp : '대상 없음', fr.length + cp > 0, !(fr.length + cp)) + cmd('reward', '포상', '금 100 · 행 ' + AP.reward) +
          cmd('move', '이동', '행 ' + AP.move) + (cp ? cmd('captive', '포로', cp + '명') : '') + '</div><div class="sec">무장</div><div class="rows">';
        var all = G.officers.filter(function (o) { return o.f === b.owner && ((o.st === 'active' && o.loc === b.id && o.unit === null) || (o.st === 'travel' && o.dest === b.id)); });
        all.sort(function (x, y) { return (isLord(y) ? 1000 : 0) + (y.ldr + y.war + y.int + y.pol) / 4 - (isLord(x) ? 1000 : 0) - (x.ldr + x.war + x.int + x.pol) / 4; });
        for (i = 0; i < all.length; i++) h += offRow(all[i], { badge: isLord(all[i]) ? '군주' : '', dim: all[i].acted || all[i].st === 'travel', rt: '<span class="' + (all[i].loy < 70 ? 'bad' : '') + '">충 ' + all[i].loy + '</span><br>' + offStatus(all[i]) });
        if (fr.length) { h += '</div><div class="sec">재야</div><div class="rows">'; for (i = 0; i < fr.length; i++) h += offRow(fr[i], {}); }
        h += '</div>';
      } else {
        h += '<div class="rows">';
        for (i = 0; i < b.plots.length; i++) {
          var fc = b.plots[i].fac;
          if (!fc) h += '<button class="row" type="button" data-a="plot" data-v="' + i + '"><span class="chip" style="background:#5a4e3c">＋</span><span class="mid"><span class="nm">빈 부지</span><span class="ds">누르면 시설을 짓습니다</span></span></button>';
          else h += '<button class="row" type="button" data-a="plot" data-v="' + i + '"><span class="chip" style="background:' + FAC[fc.t].color + '">' + FAC[fc.t].g + '</span><span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + FAC[fc.t].name + ' Lv' + fc.lv + '</span><span class="ds">' + (fc.done ? FAC[fc.t].desc : '건설 중 — ' + Math.max(1, Math.ceil((fc.need - fc.prog) / fc.rate)) + '턴 남음') + '</span></span>' + (mergeCandidates(b, i).length ? '<span class="rt hi">합병 가능</span>' : '') + '</button>';
        }
        h += '</div>';
      }
      return h + '</div>';
    }
  };
}
function done(res, msg) {
  if (!res.ok) { toast(res.msg); return false; }
  if (msg) toast(msg);
  refreshSheet(); afterAction();
  return true;
}

/* ---------- 내정 흐름 ---------- */
function flowDraft(b) {
  if (!hasFac(b, 'barracks')) return toast('병영이 필요합니다 — 시설 탭에서 지으세요');
  pickOfficers({ title: '징병 — 담당 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 3, hi: 'cha', key: function (o) { return o.cha; },
    preview: function (os) { return '예상 <b>+' + fmt(draftAmount(b, os)) + '명</b> · 치안 -' + (anyHas(os, '인덕') ? 5 : 10) + ' · 금 300'; } },
    function (os) { var r = cmdDraft(b, ids(os)); done(r, r.ok ? '징병 +' + fmt(r.n) + '명' : ''); });
}
function flowPatrol(b) {
  pickOfficers({ title: '순찰 — 담당 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 3, hi: 'war', key: function (o) { return Math.max(o.war, o.ldr); }, preview: function () { return '치안을 올립니다 · 금 100'; } },
    function (os) { var r = cmdPatrol(b, ids(os)); done(r, r.ok ? '치안 +' + r.n : ''); });
}
function flowTrain(b) {
  pickOfficers({ title: '훈련 — 담당 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 3, hi: 'ldr', key: function (o) { return o.ldr; }, preview: function () { return '주둔 병력의 기력을 올립니다'; } },
    function (os) { var r = cmdTrain(b, ids(os)); done(r, r.ok ? '기력 +' + r.n : ''); });
}
function flowProduce(b) {
  var kinds = [['spear', '창', 'smithy'], ['halberd', '극', 'smithy'], ['bow', '노', 'smithy'], ['horse', '군마', 'stable'], ['ram', '충차', 'workshop']];
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var h = dlgHead('생산 — 무엇을 만들까요') + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="opts">';
      for (var i = 0; i < kinds.length; i++) { var ok = !!hasFac(b, kinds[i][2]); h += '<button class="opt' + (ok ? '' : ' off') + '" type="button" data-a="k" data-v="' + i + '"><b>' + kinds[i][1] + '</b><small>' + (ok ? '재고 ' + fmt(b.w[kinds[i][0]]) : FAC[kinds[i][2]].name + ' 필요') + '</small></button>'; }
      return h + '</div><p class="note">창·극·노·군마는 금 300, 충차는 금 600. 지력이 높은 무장일수록 많이 만듭니다.</p></div>';
    },
    on: { k: function (el) {
      var k = kinds[+el.getAttribute('data-v')];
      if (!hasFac(b, k[2])) return toast(josa(FAC[k[2]].name, '이가') + ' 필요합니다');
      closeDialog();
      pickOfficers({ title: k[1] + ' 생산 — 담당 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 3, hi: 'int', key: function (o) { return o.int; },
        preview: function (os) { return k[0] === 'ram' ? '충차 1대 · 금 ' + (anyHas(os, '발명') ? 300 : 600) : '예상 <b>+' + fmt(produceAmount(b, k[0], os)) + '</b> · 금 300'; } },
        function (os) { var r = cmdProduce(b, k[0], ids(os)); done(r, r.ok ? k[1] + ' +' + fmt(r.n) : ''); });
    } }
  });
}
function flowTrade(b) {
  if (!hasFac(b, 'market')) return toast('시장이 필요합니다');
  var st = { dir: 'buy', v: 0 };
  function mx() { return st.dir === 'buy' ? Math.floor(b.gold / 50) * 50 : Math.floor(b.food / 6 / 50) * 50; }
  function pv() { return st.dir === 'buy' ? '금 -' + fmt(st.v) + ' → 병량 <b>+' + fmt(st.v * 4) + '</b>' : '병량 -' + fmt(st.v * 6) + ' → 금 <b>+' + fmt(st.v) + '</b>'; }
  st.v = Math.min(300, mx());
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      return dlgHead('거래 — ' + b.n) + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="opts"><button class="opt' + (st.dir === 'buy' ? ' on' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="dir" data-v="buy"><b>병량 구입</b><small>금 1 → 병량 4</small></button>' +
        '<button class="opt' + (st.dir === 'sell' ? ' on' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="dir" data-v="sell"><b>병량 판매</b><small>병량 6 → 금 1</small></button></div>' +
        '<div class="fld"><label><span>금 기준 수량</span><b id="trV">' + fmt(st.v) + '</b></label><input type="range" id="trR" min="0" max="' + mx() + '" step="50" value="' + st.v + '" data-i="v"></div>' +
        '<p class="note">보유: 금 ' + fmt(b.gold) + ' · 병량 ' + fmt(b.food) + '</p></div><div class="dlg-f"><div class="pv" id="trP">' + pv() + '</div><button class="btn pri" type="button" data-a="ok">거래</button></div>';
    },
    on: {
      dir: function (el) { st.dir = el.getAttribute('data-v'); st.v = Math.min(300, mx()); refreshDialog(); },
      v: function (el) { st.v = +el.value; $('trV').textContent = fmt(st.v); $('trP').innerHTML = pv(); },
      ok: function () { if (st.v <= 0) return toast('수량을 정하세요'); var r = cmdTrade(b, st.dir, st.v); if (r.ok) closeDialog(); done(r, r.ok ? (st.dir === 'buy' ? '병량 +' : '금 +') + fmt(r.n) : ''); }
    }
  });
}
function flowSearch(b) {
  pickOfficers({ title: '탐색 — 담당 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 1, hi: 'pol', key: function (o) { return o.pol + (o.sk === '안력' ? 50 : 0); }, preview: function () { return '재야의 인재나 숨은 재물을 찾습니다'; } },
    function (os) {
      var r = cmdSearch(b, os[0].id);
      if (!r.ok) return done(r);
      if (r.found !== undefined) { sfx('drum'); done(r, josa(off(r.found).n, '을를') + ' 찾았습니다! 등용해 보세요'); }
      else if (r.gold) done(r, '금 ' + josa(String(r.gold), '을를') + ' 찾았습니다');
      else done(r, '아무것도 찾지 못했습니다');
    });
}
function flowRecruit(b) {
  var ts = freeOfficers(b).concat(baseCaptives(b).filter(function (o) { return o.capF === b.owner; }));
  if (!ts.length) return toast('등용할 대상이 없습니다 — 탐색으로 재야 인재를 찾으세요');
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var h = dlgHead('등용 — 누구를 부를까요') + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="rows">';
      for (var i = 0; i < ts.length; i++) h += offRow(ts[i], { a: 't', badge: ts[i].st === 'captive' ? '포로' : '재야' });
      return h + '</div></div>';
    },
    on: { t: function (el) {
      var t = off(+el.getAttribute('data-v')); closeDialog();
      pickOfficers({ title: t.n + ' 등용 — 누구를 보낼까요', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 1, hi: 'cha', key: function (o) { return recruitChance(b, o, t); }, rt: function (o) { return '<b>' + recruitChance(b, o, t) + '%</b>'; },
        preview: function (os) { return '성공률 <b>' + recruitChance(b, os[0], t) + '%</b>'; } },
        function (os) { var r = cmdRecruit(b, os[0].id, t.id); if (r.ok && r.joined) sfx('drum'); done(r, r.ok ? (r.joined ? josa(t.n, '이가') + ' 합류했습니다!' : josa(t.n, '이가') + ' 거절했습니다') : ''); });
    } }
  });
}
function flowReward(b) {
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var os = baseOfficers(b).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o); }).sort(function (x, y) { return x.loy - y.loy; });
      var h = dlgHead('포상 — 금 100 · 행동력 ' + AP.reward) + '<div class="dlg-b"><p class="note">보유 금 ' + fmt(b.gold) + ' · 행동력 ' + G.factions[b.owner].ap + '</p><div class="rows">';
      for (var i = 0; i < os.length; i++) h += offRow(os[i], { a: 'r', dim: os[i].loy >= 100, rt: '<span class="' + (os[i].loy < 70 ? 'bad' : '') + '">충성 <b>' + os[i].loy + '</b></span>' });
      if (!os.length) h += '<p class="note">포상할 무장이 없습니다.</p>';
      return h + '</div></div>';
    },
    on: { r: function (el) { var r = cmdReward(b, +el.getAttribute('data-v')); if (!r.ok) return toast(r.msg); toast('충성 +' + r.n); refreshDialog(); refreshSheet(); refreshHud(); } }
  });
}
function flowMove(b) {
  var dests = facBases(b.owner).filter(function (x) { return x.id !== b.id; });
  if (!dests.length) return toast('옮겨 갈 다른 거점이 없습니다');
  pickOfficers({ title: '이동 — 보낼 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 6, key: function (o) { return o.ldr + o.war + o.int + o.pol; }, ok: '다음', preview: function (os) { return os.length + '명 선택'; } },
    function (os) {
      dests.sort(function (x, y) { return hdist(b.c, b.r, x.c, x.r) - hdist(b.c, b.r, y.c, y.r); });
      showDialog({
        render: function () {
          var h = dlgHead('이동 — 어디로 보낼까요') + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="rows">';
          for (var i = 0; i < dests.length; i++) h += '<button class="row" type="button" data-a="d" data-v="' + dests[i].id + '"><span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + dests[i].n + '</span><span class="ds">' + SIZE_NAME[dests[i].sz] + ' · 무장 ' + baseOfficers(dests[i]).length + '명 · 병력 ' + fmt(dests[i].troops) + '</span></span><span class="rt"><b>' + travelTurns(b, dests[i]) + '</b>턴</span></button>';
          return h + '</div></div>';
        },
        on: { d: function (el) { var r = cmdMoveOfficers(b, ids(os), +el.getAttribute('data-v')); if (r.ok) closeDialog(); done(r, r.ok ? os.length + '명 출발 — ' + r.turns + '턴 뒤 도착' : ''); } }
      });
    });
}
function flowCaptive(b) {
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var cs = baseCaptives(b), h = dlgHead('포로 ' + cs.length + '명') + '<div class="dlg-b"><p class="note">등용은 인재 → 등용에서 합니다. 풀어 주면 원래 세력으로 돌아갑니다.</p>';
      for (var i = 0; i < cs.length; i++) h += '<div class="row">' + porTag(cs[i]) + '<span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + cs[i].n + skTag(cs[i]) + '</span><span class="ds">' + st5(cs[i]) + '</span></span><button class="btn" type="button" data-a="rel" data-v="' + cs[i].id + '">해방</button><button class="btn" type="button" data-a="exe" data-v="' + cs[i].id + '">참수</button></div>';
      return h + '</div>';
    },
    on: {
      rel: function (el) { var o = off(+el.getAttribute('data-v')); cmdCaptive(b, o.id, 'release'); toast(josa(o.n, '을를') + ' 풀어 주었습니다'); if (!b.captives.length) closeDialog(); else refreshDialog(); refreshSheet(); },
      exe: function (el) { var o = off(+el.getAttribute('data-v')); if (el.getAttribute('data-c')) { cmdCaptive(b, o.id, 'execute'); toast(josa(o.n, '을를') + ' 참수했습니다'); if (!b.captives.length) closeDialog(); else refreshDialog(); refreshSheet(); } else { el.setAttribute('data-c', '1'); el.textContent = '정말?'; el.classList.add('pri'); } }
    }
  });
}

/* ---------- 시설 ---------- */
function flowDevelop(b, pi) {
  if (b.owner !== G.player) return;
  if (!idleOfficers(b).length) return toast('행동할 수 있는 무장이 없습니다');
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var h = dlgHead(b.n + ' — 무엇을 지을까요') + '<div class="dlg-b"><p class="note">보유 금 ' + fmt(b.gold) + ' · 행동력 ' + AP.develop + ' 소모</p><div class="lst-opt">';
      for (var i = 0; i < FAC_ORDER.length; i++) { var F = FAC[FAC_ORDER[i]], ok = b.gold >= F.cost; h += '<button class="row' + (ok ? '' : ' dim') + '" type="button" data-a="f" data-v="' + FAC_ORDER[i] + '"><span class="chip" style="background:' + F.color + '">' + F.g + '</span><span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + F.name + '</span><span class="ds">' + F.desc + '</span></span><span class="rt">금 <b>' + F.cost + '</b></span></button>'; }
      return h + '</div></div>';
    },
    on: { f: function (el) {
      var t = el.getAttribute('data-v'), F = FAC[t];
      if (b.gold < F.cost) return toast('금이 부족합니다 (' + F.cost + ' 필요)');
      closeDialog();
      pickOfficers({ title: F.name + ' 건설 — 담당 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 3, hi: 'pol', key: function (o) { return o.pol + (o.sk === '축성' ? 60 : 0); },
        preview: function (os) { var rate = 60 + Math.round((bestOf(os, 'pol') + sumOthers(os, 'pol') * 0.5) * 0.6); if (anyHas(os, '축성')) rate *= 2; return '완공까지 <b>' + Math.ceil(F.build / rate) + '턴</b> · 금 ' + F.cost; } },
        function (os) { var r = cmdDevelop(b, pi, t, ids(os)); done(r, r.ok ? F.name + ' 착공 — ' + r.turns + '턴 뒤 완공' : ''); });
    } }
  });
}
function flowFacility(b, pi) {
  var fc = b.plots[pi].fac; if (!fc) return;
  var F = FAC[fc.t];
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var cands = mergeCandidates(b, pi);
      var h = dlgHead(b.n + ' ' + F.name + ' Lv' + fc.lv) + '<div class="dlg-b"><p>' + F.desc + '</p>' + (fc.done ? '' : '<p class="note">건설 중 — ' + Math.max(1, Math.ceil((fc.need - fc.prog) / fc.rate)) + '턴 남음</p>') +
        '<div class="cmds"><button class="cmd' + (cands.length ? ' pri' : ' off') + '" type="button" data-a="merge"><b>흡수합병</b><small>' + (fc.lv >= 3 ? '최고 레벨' : (cands.length ? '금 ' + 150 * fc.lv + ' · 행 ' + AP.merge : '인접한 같은 시설 필요')) + '</small></button>' +
        '<button class="cmd" type="button" data-a="demo"><b>철거</b><small>부지를 비웁니다</small></button></div>' +
        '<p class="note">흡수합병: 바로 옆의 같은 종류 시설 하나를 흡수해 레벨을 올립니다(최대 3). 흡수된 부지는 비워집니다.</p></div>';
      return h;
    },
    on: {
      merge: function () {
        var cands = mergeCandidates(b, pi);
        if (!cands.length) return toast(fc.lv >= 3 ? '이미 최고 레벨입니다' : '바로 옆에 같은 종류의 완성된 시설이 있어야 합니다');
        closeDialog();
        pickOfficers({ title: '흡수합병 — 담당 무장', list: idleOfficers(b), max: 1, hi: 'pol', key: function (o) { return o.pol; }, preview: function () { return F.name + ' Lv' + fc.lv + ' → <b>Lv' + (fc.lv + 1) + '</b> · 금 ' + 150 * fc.lv; } },
          function (os) { var r = cmdMerge(b, pi, cands[0], ids(os)); done(r, r.ok ? F.name + ' Lv' + r.lv + '로 올랐습니다' : ''); });
      },
      demo: function (el) { if (el.getAttribute('data-c')) { cmdDemolish(b, pi); closeDialog(); toast(F.name + ' 철거'); refreshSheet(); afterAction(); } else { el.setAttribute('data-c', '1'); el.querySelector('b').textContent = '정말 철거?'; } }
    }
  });
}

/* ---------- 출진·수송 ---------- */
function flowDeploy(b) {
  var list = idleOfficers(b).sort(function (x, y) { return aiMilScore(y) - aiMilScore(x); });
  if (!list.length) return toast('행동할 수 있는 무장이 없습니다');
  if (b.troops < 100) return toast('주둔 병력이 없습니다');
  var st = { type: 'sword', ids: [], troops: 0, turns: 10, auto: true };
  function stock(t) { var wp = UNIT_TYPES[t].weapon; return wp === 'ram' ? b.w.ram : (wp ? b.w[wp] : 1e9); }
  function usable(t) { return t === 'ram' ? stock(t) >= 1 : stock(t) >= 100; }
  ['spear', 'halberd', 'bow', 'cav'].forEach(function (t) { if (usable(t) && (st.type === 'sword' || stock(t) > stock(st.type))) st.type = t; });
  function mx() {
    if (!st.ids.length) return 0;
    var m = Math.min(b.troops, cmdCap(off(st.ids[0]))), wp = UNIT_TYPES[st.type].weapon;
    if (wp && wp !== 'ram') m = Math.min(m, b.w[wp]);
    return Math.floor(m / 100) * 100;
  }
  function food() { return Math.min(b.food, Math.ceil(st.troops / 20) * st.turns); }
  function fix() { var m = mx(); if (st.auto || st.troops > m) st.troops = m; }
  function pv() {
    if (!st.ids.length) return '주장을 고르세요';
    var tmp = { type: st.type, off: st.ids, troops: st.troops, max: st.troops };
    return '공격 <b>' + Math.round(uAtk(tmp)) + '</b> · 방어 <b>' + Math.round(uDef(tmp)) + '</b> · 이동 <b>' + uMaxMp(tmp) + '</b> · 군량 <b>' + fmt(food()) + '</b>';
  }
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var h = dlgHead('출진 — ' + b.n) + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="sec">병과</div><div class="opts">', i;
      for (i = 0; i < COMBAT_TYPES.length; i++) {
        var t = COMBAT_TYPES[i], T = UNIT_TYPES[t], ok = usable(t);
        h += '<button class="opt' + (st.type === t ? ' on' : '') + (ok ? '' : ' off') + '" type="button" data-a="type" data-v="' + t + '"><b>' + T.name + '</b><small>' + (t === 'sword' ? '무기 불필요' : (t === 'ram' ? '충차 ' + b.w.ram + '대' : WEAPON_NAMES[T.weapon] + ' ' + fmt(stock(t)))) + '</small></button>';
      }
      h += '</div><div class="sec">무장 — 첫 번째가 주장, 최대 3명</div><div class="rows">';
      var ai = APT_IDX[st.type];
      for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        var o = list[i], si = st.ids.indexOf(o.id);
        h += offRow(o, { a: 'pick', sel: si >= 0, badge: si === 0 ? '주장' : (si > 0 ? '부장' : ''), rt: (ai !== undefined ? '적성 <b class="' + (o.apt.charAt(ai) === 'S' ? 'bad' : (o.apt.charAt(ai) === 'A' ? 'hi' : '')) + '">' + o.apt.charAt(ai) + '</b><br>' : '') + '지휘 ' + fmt(cmdCap(o)) });
      }
      h += '</div>';
      if (st.ids.length) {
        h += '<div class="fld"><label><span>병력 (주둔 ' + fmt(b.troops) + ')</span><b id="dpV">' + fmt(st.troops) + '</b></label><input type="range" id="dpR" min="0" max="' + mx() + '" step="100" value="' + st.troops + '" data-i="troops"></div><div class="sec">군량</div><div class="opts">';
        [5, 10, 15, 20].forEach(function (n) { h += '<button class="opt' + (st.turns === n ? ' on' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="turns" data-v="' + n + '"><b>' + n + '턴분</b><small>' + fmt(Math.ceil(st.troops / 20) * n) + '</small></button>'; });
        h += '</div><p class="note">도시 병량 ' + fmt(b.food) + '. 군량이 떨어지면 병사가 흩어집니다.</p>';
      }
      return h + '</div><div class="dlg-f"><div class="pv" id="dpP">' + pv() + '</div><button class="btn pri" type="button" data-a="ok"' + (st.ids.length && st.troops >= 100 ? '' : ' disabled') + '>출진</button></div>';
    },
    on: {
      type: function (el) { var t = el.getAttribute('data-v'); if (!usable(t)) return toast(t === 'ram' ? '충차가 없습니다 — 공방에서 생산하세요' : WEAPON_NAMES[UNIT_TYPES[t].weapon] + ' 재고가 없습니다'); st.type = t; st.auto = true; fix(); refreshDialog(); },
      pick: function (el) { var id = +el.getAttribute('data-v'), i = st.ids.indexOf(id); if (i >= 0) st.ids.splice(i, 1); else if (st.ids.length < 3) st.ids.push(id); else return toast('최대 3명입니다'); st.auto = true; fix(); refreshDialog(); },
      turns: function (el) { st.turns = +el.getAttribute('data-v'); refreshDialog(); },
      troops: function (el) { st.troops = +el.value; st.auto = false; $('dpV').textContent = fmt(st.troops); $('dpP').innerHTML = pv(); },
      ok: function () {
        var r = cmdDeploy(b, { type: st.type, ids: st.ids, troops: st.troops, food: food() });
        if (!r.ok) return toast(r.msg);
        closeDialog(); sfx('drum'); toast(uName(r.unit) + ' 출진 — 파란 칸을 눌러 이동하세요'); selectUnit(r.unit); afterAction();
      }
    }
  });
}
function flowTransport(b) {
  var list = idleOfficers(b).sort(function (x, y) { return aiMilScore(x) - aiMilScore(y); });
  if (!list.length) return toast('행동할 수 있는 무장이 없습니다');
  if (b.troops < 100) return toast('호위할 병력이 없습니다');
  var st = { id: list[0].id, troops: Math.min(1000, Math.floor(b.troops / 100) * 100), gold: 0, food: 0, spear: 0, halberd: 0, bow: 0, horse: 0, ram: 0 };
  var fs = [['troops', '병력', b.troops, 100], ['gold', '금', b.gold, 50], ['food', '병량', b.food, 500], ['spear', '창', b.w.spear, 100], ['halberd', '극', b.w.halberd, 100], ['bow', '노', b.w.bow, 100], ['horse', '군마', b.w.horse, 100], ['ram', '충차', b.w.ram, 1]];
  var on = {
    pick: function (el) { st.id = +el.getAttribute('data-v'); refreshDialog(); },
    ok: function () {
      var need = Math.ceil(st.troops / 20) * 3;
      var r = cmdDeploy(b, { type: 'trans', ids: [st.id], troops: st.troops, food: Math.max(st.food, Math.min(b.food, need)), gold: st.gold, cargo: { spear: st.spear, halberd: st.halberd, bow: st.bow, horse: st.horse, ram: st.ram } });
      if (!r.ok) return toast(r.msg);
      closeDialog(); toast('수송대 출발 — 아군 거점으로 들여보내면 물자가 합쳐집니다'); selectUnit(r.unit); afterAction();
    }
  };
  fs.forEach(function (f) { on[f[0]] = function (el) { st[f[0]] = +el.value; $('tv_' + f[0]).textContent = fmt(st[f[0]]); }; });
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var h = dlgHead('수송 — ' + b.n) + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="sec">호송 무장</div><div class="rows">', i;
      for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) h += offRow(list[i], { a: 'pick', sel: list[i].id === st.id });
      h += '</div><div class="sec">실을 것</div>';
      for (i = 0; i < fs.length; i++) { var f = fs[i], m = Math.floor(f[2] / f[3]) * f[3]; if (m <= 0 && f[0] !== 'troops') continue; h += '<div class="fld"><label><span>' + f[1] + ' (보유 ' + fmt(f[2]) + ')</span><b id="tv_' + f[0] + '">' + fmt(st[f[0]]) + '</b></label><input type="range" id="tr_' + f[0] + '" min="' + (f[0] === 'troops' ? 100 : 0) + '" max="' + m + '" step="' + f[3] + '" value="' + st[f[0]] + '" data-i="' + f[0] + '"></div>'; }
      return h + '<p class="note">수송대는 싸움에 약합니다. 병력이 먹을 군량은 최소 3턴분이 자동으로 실립니다.</p></div><div class="dlg-f"><div class="pv">행동력 ' + AP.deploy + '</div><button class="btn pri" type="button" data-a="ok">출발</button></div>';
    },
    on: on
  });
}

/* ---------- 목록·기록·메뉴 ---------- */
function showLists(tab) {
  var st = { tab: tab || 'city' };
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var h = dlgHead('목록') + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="tabs">', tabs = [['city', '거점'], ['unit', '부대'], ['off', '무장'], ['fac', '세력']], i;
      for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) h += '<button type="button" data-a="tab" data-v="' + tabs[i][0] + '"' + (st.tab === tabs[i][0] ? ' class="on"' : '') + '>' + tabs[i][1] + '</button>';
      h += '</div><div class="rows">';
      if (st.tab === 'city') {
        facBases(G.player).forEach(function (b) { h += '<button class="row" type="button" data-a="b" data-v="' + b.id + '"><span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + b.n + ' <em>' + SIZE_NAME[b.sz] + '</em></span><span class="ds">금 ' + fmt(b.gold) + ' · 병량 ' + fmt(b.food) + ' · 치안 ' + b.order + '</span></span><span class="rt"><b>' + fmt(b.troops) + '</b><br>대기 ' + idleOfficers(b).length + '명</span></button>'; });
      } else if (st.tab === 'unit') {
        var us = facUnits(G.player);
        if (!us.length) h += '<p class="note">출진한 부대가 없습니다.</p>';
        us.forEach(function (u) { h += '<button class="row' + (u.done ? ' dim' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="u" data-v="' + u.id + '">' + porTag(uLeader(u)) + '<span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + uName(u) + ' <em>' + UNIT_TYPES[u.type].name + '</em></span><span class="ds">기력 ' + u.morale + ' · 군량 ' + fmt(u.food) + (u.done ? ' · 행동 완료' : '') + '</span></span><span class="rt"><b>' + fmt(u.troops) + '</b></span></button>'; });
      } else if (st.tab === 'off') {
        var os = facOfficers(G.player).sort(function (x, y) { return (isLord(y) ? 1000 : 0) + (y.ldr + y.war + y.int + y.pol) - (isLord(x) ? 1000 : 0) - (x.ldr + x.war + x.int + x.pol); });
        os.forEach(function (o) { h += offRow(o, { badge: isLord(o) ? '군주' : '', rt: (o.unit !== null ? '출진' : G.bases[o.st === 'travel' ? o.dest : o.loc].n) + '<br><span class="' + (o.loy < 70 ? 'bad' : '') + '">충 ' + o.loy + '</span>' }); });
      } else {
        G.factions.filter(function (F) { return F.alive; }).map(function (F) { var t = 0; facBases(F.id).forEach(function (b) { t += b.troops; }); facUnits(F.id).forEach(function (u) { t += u.troops; }); return { F: F, t: t, c: facCities(F.id).length }; })
          .sort(function (x, y) { return y.c - x.c || y.t - x.t; })
          .forEach(function (x) { var L = off(x.F.lord); h += '<button class="row" type="button" data-a="f" data-v="' + x.F.id + '">' + porTag(L) + '<span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + x.F.name + (x.F.id === G.player ? ' <em>나</em>' : '') + '</span><span class="ds">도시 ' + x.c + ' · 무장 ' + facOfficers(x.F.id).length + '명</span></span><span class="rt"><b>' + fmt(x.t) + '</b><br><span class="chip" style="background:' + x.F.color + '">&nbsp;</span></span></button>'; });
      }
      return h + '</div></div>';
    },
    on: {
      tab: function (el) { st.tab = el.getAttribute('data-v'); refreshDialog(); },
      b: function (el) { var b = G.bases[+el.getAttribute('data-v')]; closeDialog(); jumpTo(b.c, b.r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.8), 0.27); selectBase(b); },
      u: function (el) { var u = UMAP[+el.getAttribute('data-v')]; closeDialog(); if (u) { jumpTo(u.c, u.r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.8), 0.27); selectUnit(u); } },
      off: function (el) { showOfficer(off(+el.getAttribute('data-v'))); },
      f: function (el) { var f = +el.getAttribute('data-v'), L = off(G.factions[f].lord), b = L.unit !== null ? null : G.bases[L.loc]; if (!b) b = facBases(f)[0]; closeDialog(); if (b) { jumpTo(b.c, b.r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.7), 0.27); selectBase(b); } }
    }
  });
}
function logRows(list) {
  var h = '';
  for (var i = list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { var e = list[i]; h += '<button class="row" type="button" data-a="j" data-v="' + i + '"><span class="mid"><span class="ds">' + e.d + '</span><span style="white-space:normal;display:block' + (e.imp >= 3 ? ';color:var(--gold);font-weight:700' : '') + '">' + e.t + '</span></span></button>'; }
  return h || '<p class="note">아직 기록이 없습니다.</p>';
}
function showLog(list, title) {
  showDialog({
    render: function () { return dlgHead(title) + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="rows">' + logRows(list) + '</div></div>'; },
    on: { j: function (el) { var e = list[+el.getAttribute('data-v')]; if (e && e.c !== undefined) { closeDialog(); clearSel(); jumpTo(e.c, e.r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.8)); } } }
  });
}
var HELP_HTML = '<div class="help">' +
  '<h3>목표</h3><p>도시 42곳을 모두 차지하면 천하통일입니다. 거점을 모두 잃으면 끝납니다.</p>' +
  '<h3>턴과 행동력</h3><p>한 턴은 10일입니다. 내정·출진 명령은 행동력을 씁니다. 행동력은 매 턴 돌아오고, 도시가 많고 군주의 매력이 높을수록 많이 돌아옵니다. 무장 한 사람은 한 턴에 한 가지 일만 합니다.</p>' +
  '<h3>도시와 시설</h3><p>도시를 누르면 명령 패널이 열립니다. 도시 둘레의 점선 칸이 부지이고, 누르면 시설을 짓습니다. 같은 시설이 붙어 있으면 흡수합병으로 레벨을 올립니다(최대 3). 조폐는 옆의 시장, 곡창은 옆의 농장 수입을 50% 늘립니다. 금은 매월 초, 병량은 1·4·7·10월 초에 들어옵니다.</p>' +
